def binary_batch(h,n,rng,deadline, max_trials=200000, batch=128, min_improve=1e-12):
    if n%2: return None, inf
    h = np.where(h>0.5,1,0).astype(float)
    # ensure sum n/2: if not, set top n/2 to 1.
    c=correlate(h,1-h,full); cm=c.max()
    best=h.copy(); bcm=cm
    # precompute E1/E0 maybe rebuild after accept? Since h changes, E matrices change only at accepted points. Could rebuild each phase.
    om=1-h[::-1]
    E1=np.zeros((n,L)); E0=np.zeros(...)
    for p in range(n):
       E1[p,p:p+n]=om[p] etc? om is length n where om[k]=1-h[n-1-k].
       E1[p, diag-p:diag-p+n] = -h
       E1[p, diag] -=1
       E0[p] = -E1? No use current: E0[p,p:p+n] = -om[p]; E0[p, diag-p:diag-p+n] = h; E0[p,diag] -=1
```

Coordinate descent exact with vectorized c update. Previous version good but could be enhanced with **random restarts and higher grid**. We'll implement a faster/more accurate version:
```
def coordinate_cd(h,n, max_passes, deadline, grid=48, refine=16, tol=1e-13, random_order=False):
    h=project_simplex
    c=correlate; bc=c.max()*dx
    diag=n-1; L=2n-1
    xs0 = np.linspace(0,1,grid)
    refs...
    improved_any=True
    passes=0
    while passes<max_passes and time<deadline:
       passes+=1
       c=...; x=h; om=1-x[::-1]
       order=np.arange(n) or rng.permutation
       any_imp=False
       for p in order:
           old=x[p]
           # precompute A length L: effect vector for δ
           A = np.zeros(L) # can allocate once and fill
           A[p:p+n] = om[p]  # δ=1 linear from h change (y unchanged)
           A[diag-p:diag-p+n] = -x  # y change linear
           # Add diagonal -δ^2 effect (concave)
           # Coordinate function F(δ)=max_l (c_l + δ A_l) - δ^2
           # Actually if x[p]+=δ, c[diag] includes -δ^2 relative old? The old c[diag] includes x_p(1-x_p); new has - x_p^2, so formula F(δ)=max_l(c_l + δ A_l) - δ^2. A as above.
           # Need choose δ ∈ [-old, 1-old] to minimize max(lags).
           # Could find 1D min by evaluating grid over δ range, refine.
           # If old near 0/1, range small.
           # Objective is max of affine minus δ^2 (same concave term). Not trivial; grid with dense sample and local refine okay.
           lo=-old; hi=1-old
           # Use transformed z∈[0,1], δ=lo+z*(hi-lo)
           # The function has curvature -1; could use golden section if unimodal? Not guaranteed. Use grid then refine best ±.
           # Maximum grid maybe 64, refine 24, then 10.
           # We can find best δ among xs.
           vals=coord_eval_fast(c,A,xs_delta,old,n) returns max c+δA -δ^2
       after each coordinate update update c,x,om
       after pass project and evaluate exact; if improved accept.
       if no improvements break
```
Vectorized `coord_eval(c,A,deltas,n)`: cand = c[None,:] + deltas[:,None]*A[None,:]; cand[:,diag]-=deltas*deltas; return cand.max(axis=1). This creates grid*n array; grid 64, n=500 => 32k per coordinate, 500 coords =>16M per pass; ok. For 10 passes 160M, okay maybe 5-10s. Previous grid 32. Use grid 64? Could be slow. We can optimize by using active lags only? Since max of c_l + δA_l -δ^2. c has length 999. We can precompute for each coordinate A? A changes each pass. Use `np.maximum.reduce`? grid*n memory ok.
But exact coordinate optimum: because term -δ^2 concave, function may be unimodal? Max of affine minus same concave. Max of affine functions is convex (in δ); subtract convex δ^2? Wait δ^2 is convex, -δ^2 is concave. Convex + concave not necessarily unimodal. But maybe. Grid okay.

def coord_eval(c,A,deltas,n):
   # deltas length m
   m = deltas.size
   cand = A[None,:]*deltas[:,None] + c[None,:]
   cand[:, n-1] -= deltas*deltas
   return cand.max(axis=1)
```

def binary_prepare(h,n):
    if n%2: return None
    # binarize by threshold top n//2
    target=n//2
    idx=np.argpartition(h, target-1)[:target] if 0<target<n else slice
    b=np.zeros(n); b[idx]=1
    return b

No closures/lambdas; top-level functions only. So `build_binary_effects` top-level.
```
def build_binary_effects(h,n):
   L=2n-1; diag=n-1
   om=1-h[::-1]
   E1=np.zeros((n,L)); E0=np.zeros((n,L))
   for p in range(n):
      E1[p,p:p+n] = om[p]
      s=diag-p
      E1[p,s:s+n] -= h
      E1[p,diag] -= 1.0
      E0[p,p:p+n] = -om[p]
      E0[p,s:s+n] = h
      E0[p,diag] -= 1.0
   ones=np.nonzero(h>0.5)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(h<=0.5)[0]
   return E1,E0,om,ones,zeros
```

For binary with om and h relation, E0 not simply -E1 because om vs h. Could compute separately. But memory/time okay. Could reduce by storing only E1 and E0 as int? We'll keep.

    c=correlate; bc=c.max()*dx

Now `slsqp_smooth`: To avoid returning worse due SLSQP stopping at infeasible? project and check.
```
def slsqp_smooth(h,n,alphas,maxiter,deadline):
   h=project_simplex(h,n); best=h.copy(); bc=...; bounds=[(0,1)]*n; cons=[...]
   for a in alphas:
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      res=minimize(smooth_obj,best,args=(n,a),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
      if res.x is not None:
         ph=project_simplex(np.asarray(res.x),n)
         pc=compute_c5(ph,n)
         if pc<bc-1e-13: best=ph; bc=pc
   return best,bc
```

Let's write robust project:
```
def project_simplex(h,n):
    h = np.asarray(h,float).clip(0,1)
    t=0.5*n
    if abs(h.sum()-t) < 1e-12: return h
    lo=-2; hi=2
    for _ in range(60): ...
    y=np.clip(h+hi,0,1)
    d=t-y.sum()
    # distribute to interior
    for _ in range(20):
       if abs(d) < 1e-12: break
       mask=(y>1e-10)&(y<1-1e-10)
       if mask.any():
          add = d/mask.sum()
          # ensure not out of bounds: compute allowed max per coord
          # If add would push some out, adjust only those within complement
          candidates = mask & (y + add >=0) & (y+add <=1)
          if candidates.any(): y[candidates]+=add
          else:
             # fallback adjust one
          d=t-y.sum()
       else:
          idx = argmax(1-y) if d>0 else argmin(y)
          delta = min(max(d, -y[idx]), 1-y[idx]) if d>0 else min(max(d,-y[idx]),1-y[idx])? Actually if d positive, delta = min(d,1-y[idx]); if d negative, delta = max(d,-y[idx]); y[idx]+=delta
    # final one-point exact
    d = t - y.sum()
    if abs(d) > 1e-15:
       if d>0: idx=int(np.argmax(1-y))
       else: idx=int(np.argmax(y))
       y[idx] = min(max(y[idx]+d,0),1)
    return y
```
But if d negative and idx value < -d? d small; okay. Need avoid non-exact due binary distribution? Sum tolerance maybe okay. We can do more robust exact via redundant final corrections:
```
for _ in range(5):
  d=t-y.sum()
  if abs(d)<1e-13: break
  idx = np.argmax(1-y) if d>0 else np.argmin(y)
  y[idx] = np.clip(y[idx]+d,0,1)
```
But if d larger than slack, may not fix. But after bisection, d small. Use this.

`smooth_val_grad`:
```
def smooth_val_grad(x,n,alpha):
    c = np.correlate(x,1-x,mode='full')
    m=c.max()
    r=alpha*(c-m); r-=r.max()
    ee=np.exp(r); z=ee.sum(); w=ee/z
    A=np.convolve(1-x,w,mode='full')
    B=np.convolve(x,w[::-1],mode='full')
    return (m+np.log(z)/alpha)*(2/n), (A-B)[n-1:2*n-1]*(2/n) ?
```
Previous `(A - B)[s:s+n]` where s=n-1. `np.convolve` length 2n-1. Good.

`binary_search` as above. Let's write top-level:
```
def build_binary_effects(h,n):
    L=2*n-1; diag=n-1
    E1=np.zeros((n,L)); E0=np.zeros((n,L)); om=1-h[::-1]
    for p in range(n):
        E1[p,p:p+n]=om[p]
        s=diag-p
        E1[p,s:s+n] -= h
        E1[p,diag]-=1
        E0[p,p:p+n] = -om[p]
        E0[p,s:s+n] = h
        E0[p,diag]-=1
    ones=np.flatnonzero(h>0.5)
    zeros=np.flatnonzero(h<=0.5)
    return E1,E0,ones,zeros
```
`om` not returned? For update maybe not need except rebuild. We can ignore om.
`binarize(h,n)` top-level:
```
def binarize(h,n):
    if n%2: return None
    t=n//2
    if t<=0 or t>=n: ...
    idx=np.argpartition(h, t-1)[:t]
    b=np.zeros(n); b[idx]=1; return b
```

def binary_search(h,n,rng,deadline):
   b=binarize(h,n); if None return None,inf
   dx=2/n; L=2n-1; diag=n-1
   best=b.copy(); c=full_c(best); bcm=c.max()*dx
   cm=bcm; no_outer=0
   trials=0; max_trials=?? based on time.
   while time.time()<deadline and no_outer<6:
       E1,E0,ones,zeros=build...
       in_no=0; outside deadline
       while time.time()<deadline and in_no<300 and trials<200000:
          m=min(128, max(1, len(ones)*len(zeros)))
          I=rng.choice(ones, size=m, replace=True)
          J=rng.choice(zeros, size=m, replace=True)
          # exclude I==J? ones zeros disjoint
          vals = c + E0[I] + E1[J]
          rows=np.arange(m)
          vals[rows, diag + I - J] += 1
          vals[rows, diag + J - I] += 1
          nm=vals.max(axis=1)
          am=int(np.argmin(nm))
          trials += m
          if nm[am] < cm - 1e-12:
             i=int(I[am]); j=int(J[am])
             # c = vals[am].copy()  (if vals still in memory)
             c = vals[am].copy()
             h init? We need update current h: b[i]=0; b[j]=1. But we lost current h? We have b variable. Update b.
             b[i]=0; b[j]=1
             cm=float(c.max())
             if cm < bcm - 1e-14:
                 best=b.copy(); bcm=cm*dx
             in_no=0
             # after accept, E matrices stale; break inner to rebuild
             break
          else:
             in_no +=1
       # after inner, maybe kick if time
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       # random perturbation: choose pairs and update c using current E (still valid before perturbation)
       k=min(3, len(ones)//2, len(zeros)//2)
       for _ in range(k):
           i=int(rng.choice(ones)); j=int(rng.choice(zeros)); 
           if i==j: continue
           c = c + E0[i] + E1[j]
           c[diag+i-j]+=1; c[diag+j-i]+=1
           b[i]=0; b[j]=1
       cm=float(c.max()); if cm<bcm: best=...
       # rebuild? next outer. If c max not improved and no improves for many, no_outer? Use a global stagnation count.
       # To avoid infinite, if in_no hit 300 and no accept, continue; after several outer with no accept, break based on best improvement.
```

`fourier_start` and `random_starts`:
```
def fourier_start(n,rng,K,scale,beta):
    x=np.arange(n)
    h=np.full(n,0.5)
    for m in range(1, K+1):
        amp=scale/(m**beta)
        ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
        h += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x/n + ph)
    return project_simplex(h,n)
```

`param_search` with Nelder-Mead:
```
def cos_basis(n,K):
    i=np.arange(n)[:,None]
    m=np.arange(1,K+1)[None,:]
    B=np.cos(np.pi*m*i/n) # modes over [0,2]: cos(2π m i/n)
    B -= B.mean(axis=1,keepdims=True)
    return B

def param_obj(params,n,B):
    h = 0.5 + B.dot(params)
    return compute_c5(project_simplex(h,n),n)
```
But `compute_c5` multiplies dx. Good. Need ensure objective finite.

yc=h0-0.5
# least squares: coeffs = np.linalg.lstsq(B, yc, rcond=None)[0]
for tr in range(tries):
   x0 = coeffs + rng.normal(0, 0.05 if tr==0 else 0.2, K)
   res=minimize(param_obj, x0, args=(n,B), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'xatol':1e-5,'fatol':1e-7})

```
def basis_cs(n,K):
   i=np.arange(n)
   rows=[]
   for m in range(1,K+1):
      rows.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*m*i/n))
      rows.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*m*i/n))
   B=np.vstack(rows)-mean
```
This can represent phase. Use K=6 (12 dim), K=10 (20). Good.

```
def markov_binary(n,p,rng):
   b=np.zeros(n); b[0]=rng.rand()<0.5
   for i in range(1,n):
      b[i]=b[i-1] if rng.rand()<p else 1-b[i-1]
   return b

def biased_markov_start(n,rng,p):
   b=markov_binary(n,p,rng)
   idx=np.arange(n)
   w=0.6 + 0.8*idx/(n-1)  # right bias? mean~1.0
   y=b*w
   return project_simplex(y,n)
```
But if b from Markov, project may produce fractional with sum exact. The right bias high values may exceed1; projection scales. Left low. This could find good basin. Try p 0.7,0.8,0.9,0.95, scale maybe.

Detailed code drafting:

```
import numpy as np, time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def project_simplex(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).clip(0,1); t=0.5*n
    if abs(h.sum()-t)<1e-12: return h
    lo,hi=-1.0,1.0
    for _ in range(55):
       m=0.5*(lo+hi)
       if np.clip(h+m,0,1).sum()<t: lo=m
       else: hi=m
    y=np.clip(h+hi,0,1); d=t-y.sum(); ran=0
    while abs(d)>1e-13 and ran<30:
       ran+=1; mask=(y>1e-10)&(y<1-1e-10)
       if mask.any() and abs(d)<0.3:
          y[mask]+=d/mask.sum()
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1-y) if d>0 else np.argmin(y))
          if d>0: y[idx]=min(1,y[idx]+d)
          else: y[idx]=max(0,y[idx]+d)
       d=t-y.sum()
    return y.clip(0,1)
```
Need ensure sum exact. At end if d still, adjust one idx. But clip may make sum off. Could final loop:
```
for _ in range(5):
  d=t-y.sum()
  if abs(d)<1e-14: break
  idx=int(np.argmax(1-y) if d>0 else np.argmin(y))
  y[idx]=float(np.clip(y[idx]+d,0,1))
return y
```
Add after while.

`corr_max(h,n)`:
```
def compute_c5(h,n):
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode="full")
    return float(c.max())*2.0/n
```

Smooth:
```
def smooth_val_grad(x,n,alpha):
    c=np.correlate(x,1-x,mode="full"); m=c.max()
    r=alpha*(c-m); r-=r.max(); ee=np.exp(r); z=ee.sum(); w=ee/z
    A=np.convolve(1-x,w,mode="full"); B=np.convolve(x,w[::-1],mode="full"); s=n-1
    return (m+np.log(z)/alpha)*(2/n), (A-B)[s:s+n]*(2/n)
def smooth_obj(x,n,a): return smooth_val_grad(x,n,a)[0]
def smooth_grad(x,n,a): return smooth_val_grad(x,n,a)[1]
def sum_fun(x,n): return x.sum()-0.5*n
def sum_jac(x,n): return np.ones_like(x)
def slsqp(h,n,alphas,maxiter,deadline):
   h=project_simplex(h,n); best=h.copy(); bc=compute_c5(best,n); bounds=[(0,1)]*n
   cons=[{"type":"eq","fun":sum_fun,"jac":sum_jac,"args":(n,)}]
   for a in alphas:
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      try:
         r=minimize(smooth_obj,best,args=(n,a),jac=smooth_grad,method="SLSQP",bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={"maxiter":maxiter,"ftol":1e-12})
         if np.isfinite(r.fun):
            ph=project_simplex(r.x,n); pc=compute_c5(ph,n)
            if pc<bc-1e-13: best=ph; bc=pc
      except Exception: pass
   return best,bc
```
Good.

```
def cd_continuous(h,n,deadline,max_passes,grid,refine,rng=None):
  h=project_simplex(h,n); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full'); bc=float(c.max())
  L=2*n-1; diag=n-1; order_base=np.arange(n)
  for _ in range(max_passes):
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    x=project_simplex(h,n); c=np.correlate(x,1-x,mode='full'); cur=float(c.max())
    om=1-x[::-1]; A=np.zeros(L); imp=False
    order=order_base if rng is None else rng.permutation(n)
    for p in order:
      old=float(x[p]); lo=-old; hi=1-old; span=hi-lo
      if span<1e-13: continue
      A.fill(0); A[p:p+n]+=om[p]; s=diag-p; A[s:s+n]-=x
      ds=np.linspace(lo,hi,grid)
      vals=c[None,:]+ds[:,None]*A[None,:]; vals[:,diag]-=ds*ds
      vv=vals.max(axis=1); bi=int(vv.argmin()); bv=float(vv[bi])
      if bv < cur - 1e-12:
        l=lo if bi==0 else ds[bi-1]; r=hi if bi==grid-1 else ds[bi+1]
        if r>l:
          d2=np.linspace(l,r,refine); v2=c[None,:]+d2[:,None]*A[None,:]; v2[:,diag]-=d2*d2
          vv2=v2.max(axis=1); b2=int(vv2.argmin())
          if vv2[b2]<bv:
             bv=float(vv2[b2]); bi2? d=d2[b2]
        d=float(ds[bi]) if ...; # need track best_d.
```
Need track `db = ds[bi]`, and if refined update `db=d2[b2]`. Use `db`.
```

      ... if ... db=float(d2[b2])
      if bv < cur - 1e-12:
         d=db-old
         c[p:p+n] += d*om[p]
         c[s:s+n] -= d*x
         c[diag] -= d*d
         x[p]=db; om[diag-p]=1-db
         cur=bv; imp=True
    xp=project_simplex(x,n); cp=float(np.correlate(xp,1-xp,mode='full').max())
    if cp < bc - 1e-12:
       h=xp; bc=cp
    elif not imp:
       break
  return h,bc*2/n
```

Binary search functions:
```
def binarize(h,n):
    if n%2: return None
    t=n//2
    if t<=0: return np.zeros(n)
    if t>=n: return np.ones(n)
    idx=np.argpartition(h,t-1)[:t]
    b=np.zeros(n); b[idx]=1.0; return b

def build_bin(h,n):
    L=2*n-1; diag=n-1; om=1-h[::-1]
    E1=np.zeros((n,L)); E0=np.zeros((n,L))
    for p in range(n):
       E1[p,p:p+n]=om[p]; s=diag-p; E1[p,s:s+n]-=h; E1[p,diag]-=1
       E0[p,p:p+n]=-om[p]; E0[p,s:s+n]=h; E0[p,diag]-=1
    ones=np.flatnonzero(h>0.5); zeros=np.flatnonzero(h<=0.5)
    return E1,E0,ones,zeros
```
`binary_search(h,n,rng,deadline,max_tries=200000, batch=128)`:
```
def binary_search(h,n,rng,deadline,max_trials=200000,batch=128):
    b=binarize(h,n)
    if b is None: return None, np.inf
    L=2*n-1; diag=n-1
    c=np.correlate(b,1-b,mode='full'); cm=float(c.max()); bcm=cm
    hcur=b.copy(); best=b.copy(); stag=0
    while time.time()<deadline and stag<5:
        if len? 
        E1,E0,ones,zeros=build_bin(hcur,n)
        if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
        inner=0; outer_improved=False
        while time.time()<deadline and inner<400 and max_trials>0:
            m=min(batch, len(ones), len(zeros))? Actually can sample with replacement so m=batch. If len small still.
            I=ones[rng.randint(0,len(ones),batch)] ...
            vals=c + E0[I] + E1[J]
            rows=np.arange(batch)
            vals[rows, diag+I-J] +=1
            vals[rows, diag+J-I] +=1
            nm=vals.max(axis=1)
            am=int(nm.argmin()); max_trials-=batch
            if nm[am] < cm - 1e-12:
                i=int(I[am]); j=int(J[am])
                c=vals[am].copy(); hcur[i]=0; hcur[j]=1; cm=float(c.max())
                if cm<bcm: best=hcur.copy(); bcm=cm; outer_improved=True
                inner=0; break
            inner+=1
        if not outer_improved: stag+=1
        else: stag=0
        # kick if time and not done
        if time.time()>deadline: break
        if inner>=400:
            k=min(3, len(ones), len(zeros))
            for _ in range(k):
               i=int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]); j=int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
               if i==j: continue
               c=c + E0[i] + E1[j]
               c[diag+i-j]+=1; c[diag+j-i]+=1
               hcur[i]=0; hcur[j]=1
            cm=float(c.max())
            if cm<bcm: best=hcur.copy(); bcm=cm
    return best,bcm*2.0/n
```
But `c=c + E0[i]+E1[j]` creates new array; assignments okay. For kick, E matrices stale after first kick (hcur changed), so should only do one kick then outer rebuild. If do multiple using same E, subsequent kicks not exact (stale). We can do only one random swap kick, or rebuild? Simpler: In outer after no inner, perform **one** perturbation swap using current E, update c/h. Since E stale after, next outer rebuild. Good. `k=1`.
If inner broke due accept, E stale; outer rebuild. Good.
If `inner<400` due deadline, no kick. Good.
`max_trials` motif: reduce but not necessary; let deadline.
`batch=128`.
Potential memory issue for n large; okay.
`stag` increments if no accept in inner; after 5 outers break. Each outer starts with a kick if no accept? In code, if no accept, inner hits400 then kick; stag increments. Next outer rebuild with perturbed h. Good. If accept, stag reset, rebuild with improved h. Good.

Starts:
```
def fourier_start(n,rng,K,scale,beta):
    x=np.arange(n); h=np.full(n,0.5)
    for m in range(1,K+1):
        h += scale/(m**beta)*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x/n + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    return project_simplex(h,n)

def markov_binary(n,p,rng):
    b=np.zeros(n); b[0]=1.0 if rng.rand()<0.5 else 0.0
    for i in range(1,n):
        b[i]=h[i-1] if rng.rand()<p else 1-h[i-1]
    return b

def biased_start(n,rng,p,scale=0.8):
    b=markov_binary(n,p,rng)
    t=np.arange(n)/(n-1)
    y=b*(0.6+scale*t)
    return project_simplex(y,n)

def smooth_start(n,rng,w):
    b=np.zeros(n); idx=rng.choice(n,n//2,replace=False) if n%2==0 else ...? for fractional n can use round.
```
If n odd, target n/2 not integer; need fractional binary? We can just make zeros length n and set `target = int(round(n/2))`. But binary not exact sum. We'll project. Use `target=n//2`? For odd, sum target half-integer, projection will adjust. We can set `b[idx]=rng.rand()`? Simpler: for random fractional start, use `b= np.where(rng.rand(n)<0.5,1,0)` and project; sum close. So:
```
def random_smooth_start(n,rng,w):
    b=(rng.rand(n)<0.5).astype(float)
    if w>1:
       ker=np.ones(w); b=np.convolve(b,ker,mode='same')*1.0/w
    return project_simplex(b,n)
```
Works for any n.
`noise_start(h,n,eps,rng)`:
```
def noise_start(h,n,eps,rng): return project_simplex(h+rng.normal(0,eps,n),n)
```

def fourier_opt(h0,n,K,rng,deadline):

       rows.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*m*i/n)); rows.append(np.sin(...))

    Bc=B.copy()? no

Can't define nested. Need top-level `param_obj(params,n,B)`. Then `fourier_opt` calls minimize. Code ~25 lines. Might be worth.
```
def param_obj(params,n,B):
    h=0.5 + B.dot(params)
    return compute_c5(project_simplex(h,n),n)

def fourier_opt(h0,n,K,rng,deadline,maxiter=120,tries=2):
    if time.time()>deadline: return None,np.inf
    i=np.arange(n); rows=[]
    for m in range(1,K+1):
       th=2*np.pi*m*i/n
       rows.append(np.cos(th)); rows.append(np.sin(th))
    B=np.vstack(rows); B-=B.mean(axis=1,keepdims=True)
    try: c0=np.linalg.lstsq(B,h0-0.5,rcond=None)[0]
    except Exception: c0=np.zeros(B.shape[0])
    best=None; bc=np.inf
    for tr in range(tries):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       x0=c0 + rng.normal(0, 0.05 if tr==0 else 0.3, B.shape[0])
       try:
          r=minimize(param_obj,x0,args=(n,B),method='Nelder-Mead',options={'maxiter':maxiter,'xatol':1e-4,'fatol':1e-7})
          h=project_simplex(0.5+B.dot(r.x),n); c=compute_c5(h,n)
          if c<bc: best,bc=h,c
       except Exception: pass
    return best,bc
```
Cost of minimize not checking deadline; maxiter 120, K=8 (16 dim) maybe 1000 eval? Nelder-Mead maxfev? We can set `maxfev=300` to limit. Options `maxfev` accepted. Use `{'maxiter':120,'maxfev':300}`. Good.
K=6 (12 dim), tries=2.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):

    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float); n0=len(h0)
    except Exception:
       n0=500; h0=fourier_start(n0,rng,8,0.4,1.0)
    if n0<100 or n0>900:
       n0=500; h0=fourier_start(n0,rng,8,0.4,1.0)
    h=project_simplex(h0,n0); n=n0
    bh=h.copy(); bc=compute_c5(bh,n); bn=n

    # transforms

    for cand in (rev, 0.5*(h+rev), q, 0.5*(h+q)):
       vv=project_simplex(cand,n); cv=compute_c5(vv,n)
       if cv<bc: bh,bc,vv? update
    if n%2==0:
       bb=binarize(h,n); cv=compute_c5(bb,n); if cv<bc: update
    def? cannot closure; inline update each.

def transfer_h(h,n,n2):
   if n2==n: return project_simplex(h,n2).copy()
   x=np.linspace(0,2,n); y=np.linspace(0,2,n2)
   return project_simplex(np.interp(y,x,h),n2)
```

```
mask=(y>0)&(y<1)
if mask.any():
   add=d/mask.sum()
   if (y[mask]+add).min()>=0 and (y[mask]+add).max()<=1: y[mask]+=add
```

for _ in range(3):
  d=t-y.sum()
  if abs(d)<1e-14: break
  idx=int(np.argmax(1-y) if d>0 else np.argmin(y))
  # choose delta not exceeding slack if possible; if d large, only partial

     delta=min(d, 1-y[idx]) else delta=max(d, -y[idx])
  y[idx]+=delta
return y
```

Let's draft run fully:

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0+max(5.0,float(budget_s)-5.0)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n=int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        n=500; h0=fourier_start(n,rng,8,0.45,1.0)
    if n<100 or n>900:
        n=500; h0=fourier_start(n,rng,8,0.45,1.0)
    h=project_simplex(h0,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n); best_n=n
    # transforms
    rev=h[::-1]
    cands=(rev, 0.5*(h+rev), 1-rev, 0.5*(h+1-rev))
    for cand in cands:
       v=project_simplex(cand,n); cv=compute_c5(v,n)
       if cv<best_c: best_h,best_c=v,cv
    if n%2==0:
       b=binarize(h,n); if b is not None:
          cv=compute_c5(b,n); if cv<best_c: best_h,best_c=b,cv
    # primary continuous
    if time.time() < deadline - 220:
       d1=min(deadline-200, time.time()+80)
       hh,cc=cd_continuous(best_h,best_n,d1,4,32,12,rng)
       if cc<best_c: best_h,best_c=hh,cc
       if time.time()<deadline-180:
          d2=min(deadline-160, time.time()+70)
          hh,cc=slsqp(best_h,best_n,(3000,30000,150000),70,d2)
          if cc<best_c: best_h,best_c=hh,cc
       if time.time()<deadline-140:
          d3=min(deadline-120, time.time()+90)
          hh,cc=cd_continuous(best_h,best_n,d3,6,48,14,rng)
          if cc<best_c: best_h,best_c=hh,cc
    # binary
    if best_n%2==0 and time.time()<deadline-120:
       d4=min(deadline-90, time.time()+120)
       bb,cb=binary_search(best_h,best_n,rng,d4,128)
       if bb is not None and cb<best_c:
          best_h,best_c=bb,cb
          if time.time()<deadline-70:
             d5=min(deadline-60, time.time()+60)
             hh,cc=slsqp(best_h,best_n,(50000,200000),60,d5)
             if cc<best_c: best_h,best_c=hh,cc
             if time.time()<deadline-40:
                d6=min(deadline-30, time.time()+50)
                hh,cc=cd_continuous(best_h,best_n,d6,2,32,10,rng)
                if cc<best_c: best_h,best_c=hh,cc
    # fourier opt
    for K in (4,7):
       if time.time()<deadline-150:
          d7=min(deadline-120, time.time()+100)
          hp,cp=fourier_opt(best_h,best_n,K,rng,d7)
          if hp is not None and cp<best_c:
             best_h,best_c=hp,cp
             if time.time()<deadline-100:
                d8=min(deadline-90, time.time()+50)
                hh,cc=cd_continuous(best_h,best_n,d8,3,36,10,rng)
                if cc<best_c: best_h,best_c=hh,cc
    # random starts
    trials=0
    while time.time()<deadline-180 and trials<18:
       trials+=1
       if trials%4==1:
          K=int(min(int(rng.randint(4,17)), max(2,best_n//2-1)))
          st=fourier_start(best_n,rng,K,float(rng.uniform(0.25,0.6)),float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.2)))
       elif trials%4==2:
          st=biased_start(best_n,rng,float(rng.choice([0.6,0.75,0.9])))
       elif trials%4==3:
          st=random_smooth_start(best_n,rng,int(rng.choice([1,3,5])))
       else:
          st=noise_start(best_h,best_n,float(rng.choice([0.05,0.1,0.2])),rng)
       d9=min(deadline-160, time.time()+60)
       hs,cs=cd_continuous(st,best_n,d9,2,24,8,rng)
       if cs<best_c:
          best_h,best_c=hs,cs
          if time.time()<deadline-130:
             d10=min(deadline-120, time.time()+50)
             hp,cp=slsqp(best_h,best_n,(20000,100000),50,d10)
             if cp<best_c: best_h,best_c=hp,cp
    # alternate n
    for nt in (400,600,350,500):
       if nt==best_n or nt<200 or nt>800 or nt%2: continue
       if time.time()<deadline-150:
          ht=transfer_h(best_h,best_n,nt); cv=compute_c5(ht,nt)
          if cv < best_c + 0.001:
             d11=min(deadline-120, time.time()+80)
             hs,cs=cd_continuous(ht,nt,d11,3,32,10,rng)
             if cs < best_c:
                best_h,best_c,best_n=hs,cs,nt
             if time.time()<deadline-80:
                d12=min(deadline-70, time.time()+50)
                hp,cp=slsqp(hs,nt,(20000,100000),50,d12)
                if cp<best_c:
                   best_h,best_c,best_n=hp,cp,nt
    # final
    if time.time()<deadline-25:
       d13=min(deadline-20, time.time()+90)
       hh,cc=slsqp(best_h,best_n,(100000,500000),100,d13)
       if cc<best_c: best_h,best_c=hh,cc
    if time.time()<deadline-8:
       d14=min(deadline-4, time.time()+60)
       hh,cc=cd_continuous(best_h,best_n,d14,3,56,14,rng)
       if cc<best_c: best_h,best_c=hh,cc
    if best_n%2==0 and time.time()<deadline-4:
       bb,cb=binary_search(best_h,best_n,rng,deadline,96)
       if bb is not None and cb<best_c: best_h,best_c=bb,cb
    final=project_simplex(best_h,best_n)
    return final, float(compute_c5(final,best_n)), int(best_n)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def project_simplex(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).clip(0.0, 1.0).copy()
    t = 0.5 * n
    if n <= 0:
        return h
    if abs(h.sum() - t) < 1e-12:
        return h
    lo, hi = -1.0, 1.0
    for _ in range(55):
        m = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if np.clip(h + m, 0.0, 1.0).sum() < t:
            lo = m
        else:
            hi = m
    y = np.clip(h + hi, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(12):
        d = t - y.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-13:
            break
        mask = (y > 1e-11) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-11)
        if mask.any():
            add = d / float(mask.sum())
            if (y[mask] + add).min() >= -1e-13 and (y[mask] + add).max() <= 1.0 + 1e-13:
                y[mask] += add
            else:
                idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - y)) if d > 0 else int(np.argmin(y))
                if d > 0:
                    y[idx] = min(1.0, y[idx] + d)
                else:
                    y[idx] = max(0.0, y[idx] + d)
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - y)) if d > 0 else int(np.argmin(y))
            if d > 0:
                y[idx] = min(1.0, y[idx] + d)
            else:
                y[idx] = max(0.0, y[idx] + d)
    for _ in range(4):
        d = t - y.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-14:
            break
        idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - y)) if d > 0 else int(np.argmin(y))
        if d > 0:
            y[idx] = min(1.0, y[idx] + d)
        else:
            y[idx] = max(0.0, y[idx] + d)
    return np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)


def compute_c5(h, n):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(c.max()) * (2.0 / n)


def smooth_val_grad(x, n, alpha):
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    m = float(c.max())
    r = alpha * (c - m)
    r -= float(r.max())
    ee = np.exp(r)
    z = float(ee.sum())
    w = ee / z
    A = np.convolve(1.0 - x, w, mode="full")
    B = np.convolve(x, w[::-1], mode="full")
    s = n - 1
    dx = 2.0 / n
    return (m + np.log(z) / alpha) * dx, (A - B)[s:s + n] * dx


def smooth_obj(x, n, alpha):
    return smooth_val_grad(x, n, alpha)[0]


def smooth_grad(x, n, alpha):
    return smooth_val_grad(x, n, alpha)[1]


def sum_con(x, n):
    return x.sum() - 0.5 * n


def sum_con_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones_like(x, dtype=float)


def slsqp_smooth(h, n, alphas, maxiter, deadline):
    h = project_simplex(h, n)
    best = h.copy()
    bc = compute_c5(best, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_con, "jac": sum_con_jac, "args": (n,)}]
    for a in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_obj, best, args=(n, a), jac=smooth_grad,
                method="SLSQP", bounds=bounds, constraints=cons,
                options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-12}
            )
            if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                ph = project_simplex(res.x, n)
                pc = compute_c5(ph, n)
                if pc < bc - 1e-13:
                    best = ph
                    bc = pc
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best, bc


def cd_continuous(h, n, deadline, max_passes, grid, refine, rng=None):
    h = project_simplex(h, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    bc = float(c.max())
    L = 2 * n - 1
    diag = n - 1
    order_base = np.arange(n)
    for _ in range(int(max_passes)):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        x = project_simplex(h, n)
        c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
        cur = float(c.max())
        om = 1.0 - x[::-1]
        A = np.zeros(L)
        imp = False
        order = order_base if rng is None else rng.permutation(n)
        for p in order:
            old = float(x[p])
            lo = -old
            hi = 1.0 - old
            span = hi - lo
            if span < 1e-13:
                continue
            A.fill(0.0)
            A[p:p + n] += om[p]
            s = diag - p
            A[s:s + n] -= x
            ds = np.linspace(lo, hi, int(grid))
            vals = c[None, :] + ds[:, None] * A[None, :]
            vals[:, diag] -= ds * ds
            vv = vals.max(axis=1)
            bi = int(vv.argmin())
            bv = float(vv[bi])
            db = float(ds[bi])
            if bv < cur - 1e-9:
                l = lo if bi == 0 else float(ds[bi - 1])
                r = hi if bi == int(grid) - 1 else float(ds[bi + 1])
                if r > l:
                    d2 = np.linspace(l, r, int(refine))
                    v2 = c[None, :] + d2[:, None] * A[None, :]
                    v2[:, diag] -= d2 * d2
                    vv2 = v2.max(axis=1)
                    b2 = int(vv2.argmin())
                    if vv2[b2] < bv:
                        bv = float(vv2[b2])
                        db = float(d2[b2])
                d = db - old
                c[p:p + n] += d * om[p]
                c[s:s + n] -= d * x
                c[diag] -= d * d
                x[p] = db
                om[diag - p] = 1.0 - db
                cur = bv
                imp = True
        xp = project_simplex(x, n)
        cp = float(np.correlate(xp, 1.0 - xp, mode="full").max())
        if cp < bc - 1e-10:
            h = xp
            bc = cp
        else:
            break
    return h, bc * (2.0 / n)


def binarize(h, n):
    if n % 2:
        return None
    t = n // 2
    if t <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if t >= n:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    idx = np.argpartition(h, t - 1)[:t]
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    b[idx] = 1.0
    return b


def build_binary_effects(h, n):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    diag = n - 1
    om = 1.0 - h[::-1]
    E1 = np.zeros((n, L), dtype=float)
    E0 = np.zeros((n, L), dtype=float)
    for p in range(n):
        s = diag - p
        E1[p, p:p + n] = om[p]
        E1[p, s:s + n] -= h
        E1[p, diag] -= 1.0
        E0[p, p:p + n] = -om[p]
        E0[p, s:s + n] = h
        E0[p, diag] -= 1.0
    ones = np.flatnonzero(h > 0.5)
    zeros = np.flatnonzero(h <= 0.5)
    return E1, E0, ones, zeros


def binary_search(h, n, rng, deadline, batch=128):
    b = binarize(h, n)
    if b is None:
        return None, np.inf
    L = 2 * n - 1
    diag = n - 1
    best = b.copy()
    c = np.correlate(best, 1.0 - best, mode="full")
    cm = float(c.max())
    bcm = cm
    hcur = b.copy()
    stag = 0
    while time.time() < deadline and stag <= 5:
        E1, E0, ones, zeros = build_binary_effects(hcur, n)
        if len(ones) == 0 or len(zeros) == 0:
            break
        inner = 0
        outer_improve = False
        while time.time() < deadline and inner < 350:
            m = int(batch)
            I = ones[rng.randint(0, len(ones), m)]
            J = zeros[rng.randint(0, len(zeros), m)]
            vals = c[None, :] + E0[I] + E1[J]
            rows = np.arange(m)
            vals[rows, diag + I - J] += 1.0
            vals[rows, diag + J - I] += 1.0
            nm = vals.max(axis=1)
            am = int(nm.argmin())
            if nm[am] < cm - 1e-9:
                i = int(I[am])
                j = int(J[am])
                c = vals[am].copy()
                hcur[i] = 0.0
                hcur[j] = 1.0
                cm = float(nm[am])
                if cm < bcm:
                    best = hcur.copy()
                    bcm = cm
                outer_improve = True
                break
            inner += 1
        if not outer_improve:
            stag += 1
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        if stag > 5:
            break
        i = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
        j = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
        if i != j:
            c = c + E0[i] + E1[j]
            c[diag + i - j] += 1.0
            c[diag + j - i] += 1.0
            hcur[i] = 0.0
            hcur[j] = 1.0
            cm = float(c.max())
            if cm < bcm:
                best = hcur.copy()
                bcm = cm
                stag = 0
    return best, bcm * (2.0 / n)


def fourier_start(n, rng, K, scale, beta):
    x = np.arange(n)
    h = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
    K = int(min(K, max(2, n // 2 - 1)))
    for m in range(1, K + 1):
        amp = float(scale) / (float(m) ** float(beta))
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        h += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x / n + ph)
    return project_simplex(h, n)


def markov_binary(n, p, rng):
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    b[0] = 1.0 if rng.rand() < 0.5 else 0.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        b[i] = b[i - 1] if rng.rand() < p else 1.0 - b[i - 1]
    return b


def biased_start(n, rng, p):
    b = markov_binary(n, p, rng)
    t = np.arange(n) / max(1, n - 1)
    y = b * (0.55 + 0.85 * t)
    return project_simplex(y, n)


def ramp_start(n, rng):
    t = np.arange(n) / max(1, n - 1)
    h = 0.25 + 0.5 * t + rng.normal(0.0, 0.15, n)
    return project_simplex(h, n)


def random_smooth_start(n, rng, w):
    b = (rng.rand(n) < 0.5).astype(float)
    if int(w) > 1:
        ker = np.ones(int(w), dtype=float)
        b = np.convolve(b, ker, mode="same") * (1.0 / int(w))
    return project_simplex(b, n)


def noise_start(h, n, eps, rng):
    return project_simplex(np.asarray(h, dtype=float) + rng.normal(0.0, float(eps), n), n)


def param_obj(params, n, B):
    h = 0.5 + B.dot(params)
    return compute_c5(project_simplex(h, n), n)


def fourier_opt(h0, n, K, rng, deadline, maxfev=300):
    if time.time() > deadline:
        return None, np.inf
    K = int(min(K, max(2, n // 2 - 1)))
    i = np.arange(n)
    rows = []
    for m in range(1, K + 1):
        th = 2.0 * np.pi * m * i / n
        rows.append(np.cos(th))
        rows.append(np.sin(th))
    B = np.vstack(rows).astype(float)
    B -= B.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    try:
        c0 = np.linalg.lstsq(B, h0 - 0.5, rcond=None)[0]
    except Exception:
        c0 = np.zeros(B.shape[0], dtype=float)
    best = None
    bc = np.inf
    for tr in range(2):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        x0 = c0 + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05 if tr == 0 else 0.25, B.shape[0])
        try:
            res = minimize(
                param_obj, x0, args=(n, B), method="Nelder-Mead",
                options={"maxiter": 120, "maxfev": int(maxfev), "xatol": 1e-5, "fatol": 1e-7}
            )
            h = project_simplex(0.5 + B.dot(res.x), n)
            c = compute_c5(h, n)
            if c < bc:
                best, bc = h, c
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best, bc


def transfer_h(h, n, n2):
    if n2 == n:
        return project_simplex(h, n2).copy()
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    y = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n2)
    return project_simplex(np.interp(y, x, h), n2)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + max(5.0, budget_s - 5.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        n = 500
        h0 = fourier_start(n, rng, 8, 0.45, 1.0)
    if n < 100 or n > 900:
        n = 500
        h0 = fourier_start(n, rng, 8, 0.45, 1.0)

    h = project_simplex(h0, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    best_n = int(n)

    rev = h[::-1]
    for cand in (rev, 0.5 * (h + rev), 1.0 - rev, 0.5 * (h + 1.0 - rev)):
        v = project_simplex(cand, n)
        cv = compute_c5(v, n)
        if cv < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = v, cv

    if n % 2 == 0:
        b = binarize(h, n)
        if b is not None:
            cv = compute_c5(b, n)
            if cv < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = b, cv

    if time.time() < deadline - 230:
        d1 = min(deadline - 210, time.time() + 80)
        hh, cc = cd_continuous(best_h, best_n, d1, 4, 32, 12, rng)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hh, cc
        if time.time() < deadline - 180:
            d2 = min(deadline - 160, time.time() + 75)
            hh, cc = slsqp_smooth(best_h, best_n, (2000, 20000, 200000), 55, d2)
            if cc < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hh, cc
        if time.time() < deadline - 130:
            d3 = min(deadline - 110, time.time() + 95)
            hh, cc = cd_continuous(best_h, best_n, d3, 6, 48, 14, rng)
            if cc < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hh, cc

    if best_n % 2 == 0 and time.time() < deadline - 110:
        d4 = min(deadline - 80, time.time() + 130)
        bb, cb = binary_search(best_h, best_n, rng, d4, 128)
        if bb is not None and cb < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = bb, cb
            if time.time() < deadline - 60:
                d5 = min(deadline - 45, time.time() + 60)
                hh, cc = slsqp_smooth(best_h, best_n, (50000, 200000), 35, d5)
                if cc < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = hh, cc
                if time.time() < deadline - 30:
                    d6 = min(deadline - 20, time.time() + 45)
                    hh, cc = cd_continuous(best_h, best_n, d6, 2, 32, 10, rng)
                    if cc < best_c:
                        best_h, best_c = hh, cc

    for K in (4, 7):
        if time.time() < deadline - 150:
            d7 = min(deadline - 120, time.time() + 80)
            hp, cp = fourier_opt(best_h, best_n, K, rng, d7, 260)
            if hp is not None and cp < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hp, cp
                if time.time() < deadline - 100:
                    d8 = min(deadline - 90, time.time() + 45)
                    hh, cc = cd_continuous(best_h, best_n, d8, 3, 32, 10, rng)
                    if cc < best_c:
                        best_h, best_c = hh, cc

    trials = 0
    while time.time() < deadline - 170 and trials < 16:
        trials += 1
        if trials % 5 == 1:
            K = int(min(int(rng.randint(4, 17)), max(2, best_n // 2 - 1)))
            st = fourier_start(best_n, rng, K, float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.60)), float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.2)))
        elif trials % 5 == 2:
            st = biased_start(best_n, rng, float(rng.choice([0.6, 0.75, 0.90])))
        elif trials % 5 == 3:
            st = ramp_start(best_n, rng)
        elif trials % 5 == 4:
            st = random_smooth_start(best_n, rng, int(rng.choice([1, 3, 5])))
        else:
            st = noise_start(best_h, best_n, float(rng.choice([0.05, 0.10, 0.20])), rng)

        d9 = min(deadline - 150, time.time() + 55)
        hs, cs = cd_continuous(st, best_n, d9, 2, 24, 8, rng)
        if cs < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hs, cs
            if time.time() < deadline - 120:
                d10 = min(deadline - 105, time.time() + 50)
                hp, cp = slsqp_smooth(best_h, best_n, (20000, 100000), 30, d10)
                if cp < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = hp, cp

    for nt in (400, 600, 350, 500):
        if nt == best_n or nt < 200 or nt > 800 or nt % 2:
            continue
        if time.time() < deadline - 140:
            ht = transfer_h(best_h, best_n, nt)
            cv = compute_c5(ht, nt)
            if cv < best_c + 0.001:
                d11 = min(deadline - 110, time.time() + 75)
                hs, cs = cd_continuous(ht, nt, d11, 3, 32, 10, rng)
                if cs < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = hs, cs, int(nt)
                if time.time() < deadline - 70:
                    d12 = min(deadline - 55, time.time() + 55)
                    hp, cp = slsqp_smooth(hs, nt, (20000, 100000), 35, d12)
                    if cp < best_c:
                        best_h, best_c, best_n = hp, cp, int(nt)

    if time.time() < deadline - 25:
        d13 = min(deadline - 18, time.time() + 75)
        hh, cc = slsqp_smooth(best_h, best_n, (100000, 500000), 40, d13)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hh, cc

    if time.time() < deadline - 8:
        d14 = min(deadline - 4, time.time() + 60)
        hh, cc = cd_continuous(best_h, best_n, d14, 3, 56, 14, rng)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hh, cc

    if best_n % 2 == 0 and time.time() < deadline - 4:
        bb, cb = binary_search(best_h, best_n, rng, deadline, 96)
        if bb is not None and cb < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = bb, cb

    final_h = project_simplex(best_h, best_n)
    final_c = float(compute_c5(final_h, best_n))
    return final_h, final_c, int(best_n)
```
